Model before rewriting:

find x: int(3..4)
find y: int(1..5)
find z: int(2..5)
find a: int(3..5)

such that

(sum([sum([x,y;int(1..)]),z;int(1..)]) / a) = 3

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
a
new variables:
  find a#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find a#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find a#sign_magnitude_3: bool
  find a#sign_magnitude_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(a#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(a#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(a#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  !(a#sign_magnitude_4)
  (!(a#sign_magnitude_4)) -> (or([!(a#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(a#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(a#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (a#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([a#sign_magnitude_1,a#sign_magnitude_2,a#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [a#sign_magnitude_1,a#sign_magnitude_2,a#sign_magnitude_3,a#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [3, 5]) >= 3,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [a#sign_magnitude_1,a#sign_magnitude_2,a#sign_magnitude_3,a#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [3, 5]) <= 5;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
